              ##

              # Execute the rule once, recording a `RuleExecution` row.
          
      11. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    11. 
            

            

              #
          
      12. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    12. 
            

            

              # Disabled rules are recorded as `"skipped"` with a `"disabled"` reason.
          
      13.

              # For unknown condition types, the rule is skipped with
          
      14.

              # `"unknown_condition_type"`.
          
      15. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    15. 
            

            

              #
          
      16. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    16. 
            

            

              # @raise [StandardError] re-raises any error after logging a failed execution
          
      17. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    17. 
            

            

              # @return [void]
          
      18. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    18. 
            
              1
            

            

              def execute!
          
      19. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    19. 
            

            

                  return log_execution("skipped", reason: "disabled") unless enabled?
          
      20.

      21. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    21. 
            

            

                  if schedule_condition?

    22. 
            

            

                    handle_scheduled_rule
          
      23. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    23. 
            

            

                  elsif attribute_value_condition?
          
      24.

                    handle_attribute_value_rule
          
      25. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    25. 
            

            

                  elsif attribute_changed_condition?
          
      26. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    26. 
            

            

                    handle_attribute_changed_rule
          
      27. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    27. 
            

            

                  else
          
      28. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    28. 
            

            

                    log_execution("skipped", reason: "unknown_condition_type")
          
      29. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    29. 
            

            

                  end
          
      30. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    30. 
            

            

                rescue StandardError => e
          
      31. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    31. 
            

            

                  log_execution("failed", error: e.message, backtrace: e.backtrace&.first(5))

                  raise

              ##

              # @return [Boolean] true when at least one asset satisfies the comparison

              def condition_met?(assets)
          
      102. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    102. 
            

            

                  return true unless attribute_value_condition?
          
      103. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    103.

              ##
          
      115.

              # Compare a raw attribute value to the configured threshold.

              #

              # @param value [Object] raw attribute value
          
      118.

              # @param operator [String] comparison operator (`"less than"`, `"greater than"`, `"equals"`)
          
      119.

              # @param threshold [Object] threshold value from configuration

              # @return [Boolean] result of the comparison
          
      121. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    121. 
            
              1
            

            

              def compare_values(value, operator, threshold)
          
      122. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    122. 
            

            

                  # Handle nil values - return false for all comparisons
          
      123. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    123. 
            

            

                  return false if value.nil?
          
      124. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    124.

                  case operator
          
      126. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    126. 
            

            

                  when "less than"
          
      127. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    127. 
            

            

                    value.to_f < threshold.to_f
          
      128. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    128. 
            

            

                  when "greater than"
          
      129. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    129. 
            

            

                    value.to_f > threshold.to_f
          
      130. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    130. 
            

            

                  when "equals"
          
      131. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    131. 
            

            

                    value == threshold
          
      132. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    132. 
            

            

                  else
          
      133. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    133. 
            

            

                    false
          
      134. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    134. 
            

            

                  end
          
      135. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    135. 
            

            

              end

              # Apply all configured actions to the given assets.

              def perform_actions!(assets)

                  Array(then_config).each do |action|

              ##
          
      160. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    160. 
            

            

              # Dispatch a single action hash to the appropriate handler.

              #

              # @param action [Hash] action configuration from `then_config`

              # @param assets [Enumerable<Asset>] assets to operate on
          
      164.

              # @return [void]

              1
            

            

              def apply_action!(action, assets = [])
          
      166. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    166. 
            

            

                  case action["action"]
          
      167. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    167. 
            

            

                  when "Calculate performance ratio"
          
      168. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    168. 
            

            

                    assets.select { |a| a.asset_type.name == "SolarPark" }.each(&:update_performance_ratio!)
          
      169. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    169. 
            

            

                  when "Update attribute"
          
      170. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    170. 
            

            

                    update_attribute_action(action, assets)
          
      171. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    171. 
            

            

                  when "Send notification"
          
      172. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    172. 
            

            

                    send_notification_action(action, assets)
          
      173. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    173. 
            

            

                  when "Log event"
          
      174. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    174. 
            

            

                    log_event_action(action, assets)
          
      175. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    175. 
            

            

                  when "Compare assets"
          
      176. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    176. 
            

            

                    compare_assets_for_deviation(action)
          
      177. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    177. 
            

            

                  when "Trigger forecast recalculation"
          
      178. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    178. 
            

            

                    Rails.logger.info "Forecast recalculation triggered for rule #{id}"
          
      179. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    179. 
            

            

                  when "Evaluate grid export strategy"
          
      180. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    180. 
            

            

                    Rails.logger.info "Grid export strategy evaluated for rule #{id}"
          
      181. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    181. 
            

            

                  end
          
      182. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    182. 
            

            

              end

    183. 
            

            

            
          
      184.

              ##
          
      185. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    185. 
            

            

              # Update a single attribute on all target assets.

              #

    187. 
            

            

              # @param action [Hash] includes `"attribute"` and `"value"` keys
          
      188. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    188. 
            

            

              # @param assets [Enumerable<Asset>] assets to update
          
      189.

              # @return [void]
          
      190.

              def update_attribute_action(action, assets)
          
      191. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    191. 
            

            

                  attr_name = action["attribute"]
          
      192. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    192. 
            

            

                  value = action["value"]
          
      193. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    193. 
            

            

            
          
      194. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    194. 
            

            

                  assets.each do |asset|
          
      195. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    195. 
            

            

                    asset.attributes_data ||= {}
          
      196. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    196. 
            

            

                    asset.attributes_data = asset.attributes_data.merge(attr_name => value)
          
      197. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    197. 
            

            

                    asset.save!
          
      198. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    198. 
            

            

                  end

    199. 
            

            

              end

              ##

              # Create a `Notification` for each asset, using an optional message template.
          
      203. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    203. 
            

            

              #
          
      204. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    204. 
            

            

              # @param action [Hash] includes `"message"` and `"severity"` keys
          
      205.

              # @param assets [Enumerable<Asset>] assets to notify about
          
      206.

              # @return [void]
          
      207.

              def send_notification_action(action, assets)
          
      208. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    208. 
            

            

                  message_template = action["message"] || "Notification triggered"
          
      209. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    209. 
            

            

                  severity = action["severity"] || "info"
          
      210. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    210.

    211. 
            

            

                  assets.each do |asset|
          
      212. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    212. 
            

            

                    Notification.create!(
          
      213. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    213. 
            

            

                      asset: asset,
          
      214. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    214. 
            

            

                      rule: self,
          
      215. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    215. 
            

            

                      severity: severity,
          
      216. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    216. 
            

            

                      message: interpolate_message(message_template, asset),
          
      217. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    217. 
            

            

                      sent_at: Time.current
          
      218. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    218. 
            

            

                    )
          
      219.

              def self.parse_time_schedule(schedule, base_time, timezone)

              def self.parse_interval_schedule(schedule, base_time, timezone)

              def self.get_delay(offset_seconds, time_since_occurrence_start_ms, schedule)
          
      75. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    75. 
            

            

                offset_ms = offset_seconds * 1000

      77.

              def self.get_time_until_next_occurrence(time_since_occurrence_start_ms, schedule)
          
      86. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    86. 
            

            

                if schedule
          
      87. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    87. 
            

            

                  if schedule.freq
          
      88. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    88. 
            

            

                    # Mirror Java's behaviour: use the actual duration between the
          
      89. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    89. 
            

            

                    # current and upcoming occurrences. Once there are no more future
          
      90. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    90. 
            

            

                    # occurrences, `upcoming` will equal `current`, so the duration
          
      91. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    91. 
            

            

                    # becomes zero and this returns a negative delay.
          
      92. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    92. 
            

            

                    return nil if schedule.current.nil? || schedule.upcoming.nil?
          
      93. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    93. 
            

            

                    duration_s = schedule.upcoming.to_i - schedule.current.to_i
          
      94. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    94. 
            

            

                    return duration_s * 1000 - time_since_occurrence_start_ms
          
      95. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    95. 
            

            

                  end
          
      96. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    96. 
            

            

                  return -time_since_occurrence_start_ms
          
      97. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    97. 
            

            

                end
          
      98. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    98. 
            

            

            
          
      99. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    99. 
            

            

                DEFAULT_REPLAY_LOOP_DURATION - time_since_occurrence_start_ms
          
      100. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    100. 
            

            

              end
          
      101. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    101.

              def parse_recurrence(rrule)

              def interval_seconds
          
      177. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    177. 
            

            

                case @freq

                when :daily then 86_400

              def occurrences_between(from_time, to_time)
          
      186. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    186. 
            

            

                occs = []
          
      187. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    187. 
            

            

                first = first_occurrence
          
      188. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    188. 
            

            

                return occs unless first
          
      189. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    189. 
            

            

                return occs if to_time < first
          
      190. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    190. 
            

            

            
          
      191. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    191. 
            

            

                step = interval_seconds
          
      192. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    192. 
            

            

                return [ first ] if step.zero?
          
      193. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    193. 
            

            

            
          
      194. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    194. 
            

            

                n_max = ((to_time.to_i - first.to_i) / step).floor
          
      195. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    195. 
            

            

                (0..n_max).each do |i|
          
      196. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    196. 
            

            

                  occ = first + i * step
          
      197. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    197. 
            

            

                  break if @count && i >= @count
          
      198. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    198. 
            

            

                  break if @until_time && occ > @until_time
          
      199. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    199. 
            

            

                  occs << occ
          
      200. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    200. 
            

            

                end
          
      201. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    201. 
            

            

            
          
      202. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    202. 
            

            

                occs
          
      203. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    203. 
            

            

              end
          
      204. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    204. 
            

            

            
          
      205. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    205.

              def next_after(time)
          
      206. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    206. 
            

            

                first = first_occurrence
          
      207. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    207. 
            

            

                return nil unless first
          
      208. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    208. 
            

            

                step = interval_seconds
          
      209. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    209. 
            

            

                return nil if step.zero?
          
      210. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    210. 
            

            

            
          
      211. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    211. 
            

            

                # If we haven't reached the first occurrence yet
          
      212. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    212. 
            

            

                return first if time < first
          
      213. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    213. 
            

            

            
          
      214. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    214. 
            

            

                elapsed = time.to_i - first.to_i
          
      215. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    215. 
            

            

                n = (elapsed / step) + 1
          
      216. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    216. 
            

            

                occ = first + n * step
          
      217. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    217. 
            

            

            
          
      218. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    218. 
            

            

                if @count && n >= @count
          
      219. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    219. 
            

            

                  return nil
          
      220. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    220. 
            

            

                end
          
      221. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    221. 
            

            

                if @until_time && occ > @until_time
          
      222. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    222. 
            

            

                  return nil
          
      223. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    223. 
            

            

                end
          
      224. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    224. 
            

            

            
          
      225. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    225. 
            

            

                occ
          
      226. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    226. 
            

            

              end
          
      227.

            end
